TL;DR: 本文提出一个面向果蝇幼虫脑三维图像的深度学习非刚性配准框架，可在单次前向推理中将脑图像快速、高精度地对齐到统一模板；相较经典和其他学习方法，在解剖标志点局部互信息、速度及低质量数据鲁棒性方面均表现最佳，并公开了模型、权重和完整流程。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 果蝇幼虫脑是神经科学研究中的重要模型。由于不同个体的脑结构存在形变，且荧光标记表达模式需要在统一解剖参考空间中比较，因此必须进行三维脑图像配准。现有经典方法通常需要对每个样本重新优化、手动调参，单个脑耗时数分钟，难以作为常规预处理步骤；同时，针对高分辨率、大体素量脑图像的学习式配准研究仍较少。

Method: 作者构建了一个训练好的深度学习非刚性配准管线，通过单次前向推理将果蝇幼虫脑体数据配准至参考模板。该工作不仅包含配准网络，还包括适用于高空间分辨率体数据的预处理流程，以及基于解剖结构锚点的评估方案。作者在独立测试集上，将方法与11种经典配准方法和7种其他学习式方法进行比较；测试数据覆盖不同成像条件和质量层级，以评估泛化性和质量退化下的鲁棒性。

Result: 提出的方法在所有比较方法中精度最高，相较最强经典基线，解剖标志点局部互信息提高23个百分点。推理速度较经典非刚性配准管线快约1至2个数量级，并且当成像质量下降时，其精度保持能力优于其他方法。模型、训练权重及完整处理流程已作为开源框架发布。

Conclusion: 深度学习配准能够有效解决果蝇幼虫脑高分辨率三维图像的快速、自动和鲁棒对齐问题。该框架在精度、速度及跨成像质量稳定性方面均优于所比较的经典与学习式基线，有望将脑图像配准转变为可日常使用的标准预处理步骤。

Abstract: The larval stage of Drosophila melanogaster is a compact model system for neuroscience whose genetic toolkit allows fluorescent markers to be expressed in defined neural populations, and comparing the resulting expression patterns across animals requires every brain to be registered into a shared anatomical reference space. Existing pipelines for this task are predominantly based on classical registration methods, which perform a new optimization for each volume, often require per-case parameter tuning, and can take minutes per brain, limiting their use as a routine preprocessing step. We present a trained deep registration pipeline that deformably aligns a larval brain to a reference template in a single forward pass at high spatial resolution, on volumes that hold several times more voxels than those learned 3D registration is normally reported on, together with the preprocessing and anatomy-anchored evaluation pipeline required to apply it. Against eleven classical and seven further learned baselines on a held-out collection acquired with different acquisition and quality strata, the proposed pipeline is the most accurate, improving on the strongest classical baseline by 23 percentage points of anatomical landmark-local mutual information. It registers a volume one to two orders of magnitude faster than the classical deformable pipelines, and it retains more of its accuracy than any other method as acquisition quality degrades. The network, its trained weights and the full pipeline are released as the open-source deep larval brain registration framework: https://github.com/agentdr1/deep-larval-brain-reg

</details>


### [33] [From Evaluation to Enhancement: Benchmarking and Improving Think-with-Video Reasoning for Video Generative Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.11242)
*Meng Luo,Yicheng Liu,Jiahao Wang,Yuanxing Zhang,Xin Tao,Pengfei Wan,Kun Gai,Hao Fei*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出VWG-Bench视频世界通用能力基准和Vid-PRE提示词推理增强器，用于评估并提升视频生成模型的规则推理、物理一致性与目标导向能力。结果表明，现有模型视觉生成质量较高，但在逻辑密集和规则约束任务上存在明显不足；Vid-PRE无需修改生成器架构即可显著改善推理表现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有视频生成模型虽然能够生成视觉上吸引人且时间连贯的视频，但尚不清楚它们是否真正具备类似“思考视频”的能力，例如执行符号规则、遵守物理规律和实现明确目标。已有评测往往将视觉质量与认知正确性混为一谈，缺乏对视频认知能力的系统、细粒度诊断。因此，本文旨在建立覆盖多种推理维度的评测体系，并探索一种不改动生成模型架构即可提升其推理能力的方法。

Method: 首先提出VWG-Bench（Video World Generalist Benchmark），覆盖9个推理维度和38项细粒度任务。设计三级VLM-as-Judge评测协议，分别独立评估视频层面的流畅性、任务层面的规则遵循，以及样本层面的目标实现。其次提出Vid-PRE（Video Prompt Reasoner and Enhancer），利用专门的视觉语言模型承担推理工作，将输入改写为简洁且考虑约束的提示词。该方法通过纯文本奖励进行强化学习训练，避免使用视频级奖励信号所带来的不稳定性，并可与不同视频生成器组合使用。

Result: 对多种主流视频生成模型的评测显示，模型在渲染质量方面表现较强，但在逻辑密集和规则约束任务上持续失败，说明视觉质量与认知正确性之间存在显著差距。Vid-PRE在多个视频生成器上均带来明显的推理能力提升，且不需要修改生成器的网络架构。数据集和代码已公开。

Conclusion: VWG-Bench为视频生成模型的“思考视频”能力提供了系统化、可诊断的评测工具，Vid-PRE则提供了一种可扩展、模型无关的推理增强路径。研究表明，当前模型仍主要擅长生成而非遵循复杂规则或实现意图，但通过专门的提示推理模块可以有效缩小这一差距。

Abstract: Video generation has advanced to produce visually compelling and temporally coherent results. Yet, whether these models can genuinely think with video--executing symbolic rules, respecting physical laws, and pursuing intentional goals--remains an open question. Existing benchmarks only partially address this, often conflating visual quality with cognitive correctness. We introduce VWG-Bench (Video World Generalist Benchmark), a comprehensive benchmark spanning 9 reasoning dimensions and 38 fine-grained tasks. To enable precise diagnosis, we design a three-level VLM-as-Judge protocol that independently assesses video-level fluency, task-level rule adherence, and sample-level goal realization. Evaluations of leading models reveal a striking gap: while models achieve strong rendering scores, they consistently fail on logic-heavy and rule-constrained tasks. To address this, we propose Vid-PRE (Video Prompt Reasoner and Enhancer), a model-agnostic prompt rewriter that offloads the cognitive burden of reasoning to a dedicated VLM. Trained via reinforcement learning with purely text-based rewards, Vid-PRE produces concise, constraint-aware prompts without the instability of video-level reward signals. Experiments show that Vid-PRE yields substantial reasoning improvements across multiple generators without architectural modifications. Together, VWG-Bench and Vid-PRE offer a rigorous diagnostic lens and a scalable path toward true think-with-video capabilities. All data and code are publicly available at https://huggingface.co/datasets/KlingTeam/VWG-Bench.

</details>


### [34] [Uncertainty DMD: Restoring Diversity in Few-Step Autoregressive Video Distillation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.11265)
*Zixuan Duan,Xunzhi Xiang,Yabo Chen,Xin Zhang,Changhan Liu,Haibin Huang,Chi Zhang,Qi Fan,Xuelong Li*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文研究少步蒸馏自回归视频生成中的多样性坍塌问题，提出无需改动网络结构的 Uncertainty DMD，通过首块时间步扰动和后续块随机缓存写入，在训练与推理阶段注入并保留不确定性，从而改善视频多样性与运动动态，同时维持相近的单样本视觉质量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 少步蒸馏能够提升自回归视频生成效率，但 Distribution Matching Distillation（DMD）存在模式寻优偏置，容易使同一提示词下不同噪声样本生成高度相似的视频，并削弱运动变化。本文希望解释这种多样性坍塌在自回归生成中的具体传播机制，并以轻量方式恢复随机性与时间动态。

Method: 作者分析发现，多样性坍塌具有结构化不确定性坍塌特征：DMD首先将不同噪声映射到近似相同的首个视频块；随后，自回归模型中的确定性缓存将该坍塌状态传播到后续所有视频块，使局部的首块随机性损失演变为全局时间变化受抑制。基于此提出 Uncertainty DMD：1）对首个视频块施加时间步扰动，以提升首块生成多样性；2）对后续视频块采用随机缓存写入机制，使不确定性能够保留在自回归条件中。该方法无需修改网络架构，仅增加轻量扰动操作，并在训练和推理阶段使用相同的扰动机制。

Result: 实验表明，Uncertainty DMD能够稳定提升生成视频的多样性和运动动态，同时保持与基线相近的单样本视觉质量，说明其并非单纯以牺牲画质换取随机性。摘要未提供具体数据、数据集或评价指标，因此无法进一步量化提升幅度。

Conclusion: 本文指出，DMD在自回归视频生成中的问题并非简单的整体随机性下降，而是从首个视频块开始、经由确定性缓存逐步扩散的结构化不确定性坍塌。通过在首块和后续缓存阶段分别注入与保留不确定性，Uncertainty DMD以较低成本改善了视频生成的多样性和时序运动表现，具有较好的工程实用性。

Abstract: Few-step distillation improves the efficiency of autoregressive (AR) video generation, but often causes diversity collapse: under the same prompt, different noise samples tend to produce highly similar videos with weakened motion dynamics. We analyze this degradation in Distribution Matching Distillation (DMD)-distilled AR video generators and find that, in the autoregressive setting, it takes the form of a structured uncertainty collapse: the mode-seeking bias of DMD maps different noise samples to nearly identical first chunks, and the deterministic AR cache then propagates this collapsed state to all subsequent chunks, turning a local loss of stochasticity at the rollout root into a global suppression of temporal variation. Based on this analysis, we propose Uncertainty DMD, a simple uncertainty-injection framework that restores stochasticity at two key stages of AR generation: a timestep perturbation for the first chunk to increase first-chunk diversity, and a stochastic cache-writing mechanism for later chunks to preserve uncertainty in autoregressive conditioning. The method requires no architectural changes and introduces only lightweight perturbation operations. The same perturbation mechanisms are used during both training and inference. Experiments show that Uncertainty DMD consistently improves diversity and motion dynamics while maintaining comparable per-sample visual quality.

</details>


### [35] [Improving Faint Object Detection for Space Situational Awareness with Variational Autoencoders](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.11269)
*Angela Cratere,Luca Ghilardi,Vishnu Reddy,Francesco Dell'Olio,Charalampos S. Kouzinopoulos,Roberto Furfaro*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出一个面向光学空间态势感知的深度学习预处理流程：利用Tiny-U-Net生成恒星掩膜，再通过带部分卷积的变分自编码器astro-VAE重建无恒星背景，并结合shift-and-stack提升低信噪比运动目标的可检测性。真实地基望远镜X-GEO观测结果表明，该方法能够较高保真地抑制恒星和背景不均匀性，同时保留运动目标。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 光学SSA图像中的微弱运动目标通常具有低信噪比，尤其在地月空间或X-GEO区域，密集恒星场、结构化天空背景和散射月光会显著降低传统检测算法的性能。因此，需要一种能够自动移除固定恒星源、重建背景并减少背景不均匀性的学习型预处理方法。

Method: 流程包括两个核心模块：1）轻量级Tiny-U-Net分割网络，用于生成恒星掩膜；2）带部分卷积的变分自编码器astro-VAE，学习天文背景的统计分布，并对被掩膜区域进行具有上下文感知能力的图像修复，从而生成无恒星背景图。随后，将重建背景用于抑制固定源和背景起伏，并与shift-and-stack运动目标检测方案集成。方法在真实地基望远镜X-GEO观测数据上进行验证。

Result: 实验结果显示，astro-VAE能够以较高保真度重建去除恒星后的背景，同时保留运动目标信息；作为检测前处理步骤，该方法显著提升了微弱运动目标的可检测性。论文摘要未提供定量指标、基线方法对比数值或统计显著性结果。

Conclusion: 该方法证明了深度学习背景重建可作为光学SSA微弱运动目标检测的有效数据驱动预处理策略，尤其适用于恒星密集、背景复杂的X-GEO观测场景。其主要贡献在于将自动恒星分割、上下文背景修复与shift-and-stack检测结合起来。

Abstract: We present a deep-learning pipeline for enhancing the detection of faint moving objects in optical space situational awareness (SSA) imagery through automated star removal and background reconstruction. Detecting low signal-to-noise ratio (SNR) objects remains extremely challenging in optical observations, particularly in the cislunar (X-GEO) environment, where structured sky backgrounds, dense stellar fields, and scattered moonlight significantly degrade the performance of classical detection algorithms. To address this problem, the proposed pipeline combines a lightweight segmentation network (Tiny-U-Net) to generate stellar masks with a partial-convolution variational autoencoder (astro-VAE), designed to learn the statistical distribution of astronomical backgrounds and perform context-aware inpainting of masked regions. The reconstructed background maps can then be used as a preprocessing step to suppress fixed sources and background inhomogeneities prior to detection. As a proof of concept, the approach is integrated with a shift-and-stack scheme and evaluated on real ground-based telescope observations targeting the X-GEO region. Results demonstrate that the method reconstructs star-free backgrounds with high fidelity, while preserving moving targets and significantly enhancing detectability, thereby providing an effective data-driven preprocessing strategy for faint moving-object detection in optical SSA scenarios.

</details>


### [36] [Order-Aware 2.5D Multiple Instance Learning for Preoperative MRI-Based Perineural Invasion Risk Assessment in Intrahepatic Cholangiocarcinoma](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.11271)
*Hyunsu Go,Youngung Han,Kyeonghun Kim,Jinyong Jun,Junbeom Lee,Dohyun Kweon,Yului Jeong,Suah Park,Sungha Park,Anna Jung,Woo Kyoung Jeong,Ken Ying-Kai Liao,Hyuk-Jae Lee,Nam-Joon Kim*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出顺序感知的切片级多实例学习框架（OAS-MIL），利用肿瘤中心MRI裁剪区域中连续轴位切片构成的有序重叠2.5D slab序列，在仅有患者级标签的弱监督条件下预测肝内胆管癌的神经周围侵犯（PNI）。模型结合置换不变集合注意力与双向序列注意力，在患者级五折分层交叉验证中取得平均AUROC 0.770，并优于所比较的体积模型和MIL基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 肝内胆管癌中的神经周围侵犯（PNI）是重要的不良组织病理学标志，但通常只能在手术切除后确认，限制了术前风险评估和治疗规划。术前T2加权MRI可能包含与PNI相关的影像信息，但研究数据只有患者级标签，缺乏切片级或体素级标注，因此需要一种能够利用弱监督信息、同时保留MRI轴位切片空间顺序的预测方法。

Method: 提出Order-Aware Slab Multiple Instance Learning（OAS-MIL）。首先，以肿瘤为中心裁剪MRI区域，并将连续轴位切片组织为有序、相互重叠的2.5D slab序列。随后使用共享编码器提取每个slab的特征，并通过两条聚合分支建模信息：一条是对输入排列不敏感的集合注意力分支，另一条是利用前后方向上下文的双向序列注意力分支。训练和评估采用患者级标签分层的五折交叉验证，以避免切片级数据泄漏。

Result: OAS-MIL在患者级PNI预测中取得平均AUROC 0.770，优于研究中评估的体积模型和其他多实例学习基线。结果表明，在弱监督MRI预测任务中，保留轴位切片顺序能够提供有效的归纳偏置。摘要未报告敏感度、特异度、置信区间或外部验证结果。

Conclusion: 基于有序2.5D slab序列的OAS-MIL能够在仅有患者级标签的条件下，从术前T2加权MRI中预测肝内胆管癌PNI，并取得较有竞争力的性能。研究支持将轴向顺序信息纳入弱监督多实例学习模型；但其临床泛化能力仍需通过更大规模、多中心和外部队列验证。

Abstract: Perineural invasion (PNI) is an adverse histopathologic marker in intrahepatic cholangiocarcinoma (ICC), but it is usually confirmed only after resection. Preoperative T2-weighted MRI may provide noninvasive imaging cues predictive of PNI, although labels are available only at the patient level without slice- or voxel-level annotations. We propose Order-Aware Slab Multiple Instance Learning (OAS-MIL), a weakly supervised framework for patient-level PNI prediction. Each tumor-centered MRI crop is represented as an ordered sequence of overlapping 2.5D slabs formed from contiguous axial slices. A shared encoder extracts slab-level features, which are aggregated by a permutation-invariant set-attention branch and a bidirectional sequence-attention branch. Using five-fold label-stratified cross-validation at the patient level, OAS-MIL achieved a mean AUROC of 0.770, outperforming the evaluated volumetric and MIL baselines. These results suggest that axial order provides a useful inductive bias for weakly supervised PNI prediction from MRI.

</details>

### [37] [Data-Efficient Language Modeling: From Frontier Advancement to Principle-Guided Model Improvement](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.10702)
*Shuxing Yang,Kaihao Zhu,Junjie Yang,Rui Zhao,Junyao Wu,Yize Wang,Wenhao Li,Fujia Chen,Taowen Deng,Shenzhan Hong,Yaqi Li,Zichen Li,Jincheng Mi,Yuang Pan,Hongsheng Chen,Yihao Yang*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文介绍 Qiushi Engine 在严格小规模数据与训练预算下，通过三阶段自主研究探索数据高效学习、泛化保持与递归式研究改进；其改进模型在公开 Strict-Small 快照中取得最高 Overall 分数。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在极有限的语料规模和累计词呈现预算下，模型必须同时学会利用上下文、迁移到未见输入，并保留已有能力。论文旨在发现可验证的数据高效学习原则，并将这些原则用于模型改进。

Method: 研究分为三阶段：第一阶段通过紧凑重述、预算再投资和残差增量学习构建前沿模型；第二阶段在受控任务中比较精确重复与对齐重述，分析上下文使用、目标关系、预测窗口，以及熟悉性能恢复与未见输入泛化之间的差异；第三阶段保留源文本、遮蔽更多局部线索、监督选定目标，并保持通常被遮蔽输入上的预测，以进行原则指导的继续训练。研究还考察了压缩、关系锚点、共享表示和测量问题。

Result: 第二阶段表明，精确重复和对齐重述会产生不同的上下文利用模式；恢复熟悉任务性能并不保证模型能在未见输入上继续使用已学习的计算。第三阶段产生的两个同源继续训练种子，在九项指标的综合结果上优于普通继续训练。Overall 分数由 42.02 提升至 42.25，第二代模型在 2026 年 9 月 8 日公开 Strict-Small 快照中达到最高 Overall。

Conclusion: 论文提出并支持一项可检验的数据高效学习原则：围绕预测所需的上下文依赖组织经验，并分别设计可见信息、监督信号和能力保持机制，同时独立测试学习、泛化与保持。整体工作体现了 Research RSI，即研究过程通过科学理解和方法创新递归改进。

Abstract: Learning from limited text requires models to use context, generalize to new inputs, and retain useful capabilities. Qiushi Engine conducted a long-horizon, end-to-end autonomous research program on BabyLM 2026 Strict-Small, within 10 million corpus words and 100 million cumulative word presentations. Three stages connected frontier advancement, principle discovery, and principle-guided model improvement. Stage I combined compact restatements, budget reinvestment, and residual incremental learning to build a frontier model. Stage II found that exact repetition and aligned restatement produce different patterns of context use, depending on target relations and prediction windows. In controlled tasks, recovering familiar performance did not ensure that unseen inputs could still use learned computations. These findings support a testable data-efficient learning principle: organize experience around the contextual dependencies needed for prediction; separately design visible information, supervision, and preservation; test learning, generalization, and retention. Stage III retained source text, masked more local clues, supervised selected targets, and preserved predictions on ordinarily masked inputs. Two continuation seeds from the same parent outperformed ordinary continuation on the complete nine-metric aggregate. Overall rose from 42.02 to 42.25 across two generations; the second achieved the highest Overall in the public Strict-Small snapshot of 8 September 2026. Further studies addressed compression, relational anchors, shared representations, and measurement. Models are available on Hugging Face; code and research records accompany the GitHub repository. Together, these stages illustrate Research RSI: recursive self-improvement of the research process. Scientific understanding and method innovations change subsequent questions and designs; new experiments test and refine them.

</details>


### [38] [NCP-ArchPreview Technical Report: Moving towards Latent Space Language Models through Next Concept Prediction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.10715)
*The Intern-NCP Team,:,Jiaqi Cao,Chiyu Chen,Shuang Cheng,Xu Cheng,Beiya Dai,Yufan Feng,Kewen Ge,Ruijun Ge,Jiayi Huang,Yang Jiao,Dahua Lin,Zhouhan Lin,Yifan Liu,Yuliang Liu,Biqing Qi,Mowen Ruan,Junzhe Shen,Yunchong Song,Hao Sun,Zhongbo Tian,Yixuan Wang,Rubin Wei,Jiaxin Xiong,Kangyu Yang,Qian Yao,Qi Zhang,Bowen Zhou*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: NCP-ArchPreview 是一种结合标准下一词预测（NTP）与下一概念预测（NCP）的潜空间语言模型。它从隐藏状态构建产品量化概念词表，并通过概念模块预测未来跨词元概念，再将概念反馈至词元生成。该模型在8.9B参数、5.73T词元规模上训练，显示出更高的训练效率、下游性能和潜空间复用价值。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 标准自回归语言模型主要进行下一词预测，难以显式建模跨多个词元的高层语义概念，可能导致训练效率和推理规划能力受限。论文旨在引入更具挑战性的概念级预测目标，在保留标准词元级生成能力的同时，提升模型的表示学习、样本效率、下游任务性能和潜空间可复用性。

Method: 模型联合训练NTP和NCP两个目标。首先从模型隐藏状态中构建产品量化的离散概念词表，形成潜空间；随后利用独立的Concept Module预测未来概念，并将预测概念反馈到词元级生成过程。整体架构端到端训练，并扩展至8.9B参数、使用Dolma-3的5.73T词元进行预训练。此外，论文评估了潜空间在轻量领域适配和推测解码中的应用：仅更新17M参数的VQ模块进行适配，并将概念表示注入DFlash2 drafter。

Result: 相较OLMo-3-7B，模型仅消耗51.3%的总训练词元即可达到其最终预训练损失；完整预训练后，下游宏平均成绩提升2.45分，GSM8K提升5.99分。受控实验表明，性能提升分别来自潜空间架构和NCP目标。相较参数规模严格对齐的8.9B基线，模型使用85%的标准计算量即可接近其训练损失。预训练后的潜空间仍具实用价值：仅更新17M参数的VQ模块即可提供轻量领域适配接口；将概念表示注入DFlash2 drafter后，平均接受长度提升4.17%，且额外开销可忽略。

Conclusion: 论文表明，在标准下一词预测之外加入下一概念预测，并通过离散潜空间连接高层概念与词元生成，可以同时改善训练效率、下游任务性能和模型扩展能力。NCP-ArchPreview不仅在大规模预训练阶段有效，其潜空间还可用于低成本领域适配和推测解码增强，说明潜空间表示具有较强的后训练复用价值。

Abstract: We introduce NCP-ArchPreview, a latent-space language model that pushes autoregressive pretraining beyond standard next-token prediction (NTP). Alongside NTP, the model learns through Next Concept Prediction (NCP) to predict discrete concepts that span multiple tokens, introducing an explicit and more challenging concept-level objective while preserving standard token-level autoregressive generation. NCP-ArchPreview builds a latent space by constructing a product-quantized concept vocabulary directly from its hidden states, and subsequently learns to predict future concepts via a dedicated Concept Module. These predicted concepts are then fed back to the token level to guide subsequent generation, with NTP and NCP trained jointly end-to-end. We scale this architecture to 8.9B parameters and train it on 5.73T tokens from the Dolma-3 dataset, marking the largest demonstration of a latent-space language model to date. Remarkably, by consuming only 51.3% of the total training tokens, NCP-ArchPreview achieves the final pretraining loss of OLMo-3-7B. Following full pretraining, it outperforms OLMo-3-7B by 2.45 points on the downstream macro-average, including a notable 5.99-point gain on GSM8K. Controlled experiments isolate a clear progression of performance gains stemming from both the latent architecture and the NCP objective. Furthermore, utilizing only 85% of the standard computation, NCP-ArchPreview approaches the training loss of a strictly parameter-aligned 8.9B baseline. The learned latent space remains highly valuable after the pretraining stage: updating just the 17M-parameter VQ module yields a novel, lightweight interface for domain adaptation, while a simple injection of concept representations into a DFlash2 drafter improves the mean accepted length by 4.17% with negligible overhead.

</details>


### [39] [CMNIE: An Information Extraction Benchmark for Chinese Military News](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.10722)
*Yan Yu,Mengna Zhu,Zhenyu Song,Hao Yang,Haiwen Chen,Mao Wang*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: CMNIE是一个面向中文军事新闻的联合信息抽取基准，统一标注事件、论元、实体及关系，并系统评测传统模型、零样本大语言模型和微调大模型。结果表明，关系抽取与事件论元边界精确匹配仍是主要难点。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有领域资源主要关注文档级事件标注，对事件触发词、事件论元、命名实体和实体关系的联合建模支持不足，难以满足专业新闻中的结构化信息抽取、决策支持和知识库构建需求。因此，作者构建统一模式下的综合基准。

Method: 收集并人工标注13,000条公开中文军事新闻实例，定义7类事件、10类论元角色、7类实体和8类关系，形成统一领域模式。在共享测试集上比较监督式信息抽取模型、零样本大语言模型以及基于大语言模型微调的抽取方法，重点考察模式遵循、联合抽取和事件论元跨度的精确匹配。

Result: CMNIE对现有方法仍具有较高挑战性，其中关系抽取表现较弱，事件论元的精确跨度匹配尤其困难。零样本大语言模型通常能够识别相关语义单元，但经常无法与金标准保持完全一致的边界。

Conclusion: CMNIE填补了中文军事新闻中多任务联合信息抽取基准的不足，可用于研究统一模式遵循、精确跨度匹配及专业新闻场景下的结构化抽取，并为后续模型改进提供标准化评测平台。

Abstract: Structured extraction from Chinese military news supports intelligence analysis, decision-making, and knowledge base construction. However, existing resources provide limited support for joint informa?tion extraction in this domain, especially when events, event arguments, entities, and relations must be modeled together. We present CMNIE, an information extraction benchmark for Chinese military news. Extend?ing military-domain resources beyond document-level event annotations, CMNIE jointly annotates event triggers, event arguments, named enti?ties, and entity relations under a unified domain schema. The dataset contains 13,000 instances collected from public Chinese military news, with manual annotations for 7 event types, 10 argument roles, 7 entity types, and 8 relation types. We evaluate supervised IE models, zero-shot large language models, and fine-tuned LLM-based extraction methods on a shared test set. Experimental results show that CMNIE remains chal?lenging, especially for relation extraction and exact matching of event?argument spans; zero-shot LLMs often identify relevant semantic units but fail to match gold span boundaries exactly. CMNIE provides a stan?dardized benchmark for studying schema adherence, exact span match?ing, and joint structured extraction in specialized Chinese news.

</details>


### [40] [Think Before You Link: Rarity, Reasoning, and Retrieval in Multilingual Entity Linking](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.10745)
*Parinthapat Pengpun,Simran Khanuja,Graham Neubig*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 论文研究多模态实体链接中的稀有实体问题，提出基于知识图谱结构指标的新型稀有度定义，并设计一种无需训练、结合迭代检索与视觉语言模型推理的框架。在MERLIN五语种基准上，该方法整体准确率提升6.9%，稀有实体切片最高提升23.3%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有多模态实体链接系统在稀有实体上性能显著下降，但既有研究主要使用页面浏览量等流行度指标衡量稀有性，无法充分反映实体的文档完整性和知识图谱连接程度，也难以覆盖不同类型的稀有实体及其特有失败模式。因此，论文旨在构建更全面的稀有实体评估体系，并提升系统对这些实体的链接能力。

Method: 首先引入知识图谱结构指标，从实体的记录充分程度和图谱连接性等角度定义稀有实体，构建多个稀有实体切片。随后提出一个无需训练的框架：由具备推理能力的视觉语言模型在Wikipedia上进行迭代式搜索、证据收集与推理。通过受控实验分别评估推理和检索的作用，并在MERLIN多语言多模态实体链接基准及新发布的MERLIN-Rare测试切片上进行评测。

Result: 不同稀有度定义揭示了不同的系统缺陷；现有最先进系统在各类稀有实体切片上的准确率下降15.4%至39.9%。实验表明，单独推理不能显著提升稀有实体准确率；仅使用检索虽能改善稀有实体表现，却可能损害总体准确率；检索与推理结合效果最佳。在MERLIN五种语言上，最佳系统整体领先当前最优方法6.9%，在稀有实体切片上最高提升23.3%。

Conclusion: 论文证明，稀有实体不应仅由流行度衡量，知识图谱结构指标能够发现更丰富且具有不同挑战的稀有实体。动态检索与视觉语言模型推理的协同是提升稀有实体链接性能的关键，其中检索负责获取外部证据，推理负责整合和利用证据。MERLIN-Rare为面向稀有实体的针对性评测提供了新的资源。

Abstract: Multimodal entity linking grounds entity mentions in text and images to knowledge-base entries. These systems degrade on rare entities, but prior work measures rarity primarily through popularity-based metrics such as pageviews. We broaden this view using knowledge-graph structural metrics that capture how well an entity is documented and connected. These metrics identify many rare entities that popularity metrics miss. Across the resulting rare-entity slices, state-of-the-art accuracy drops by 15.4-39.9%, showing that different rarity definitions expose different failure modes. To address these failures, we introduce a simple, training-free framework in which a reasoning-capable vision-language model iteratively searches and reasons over Wikipedia, gathering evidence dynamically. Controlled experiments show that reasoning and retrieval are complementary. Reasoning alone does not significantly improve accuracy on rare entities. Retrieval without reasoning improves rare-entity accuracy but can hurt overall accuracy. Their combination performs best. On MERLIN, a multilingual multimodal entity linking benchmark over five languages (Hindi, Indonesian, Japanese, Tamil, Vietnamese), our best system improves over the state of the art by 6.9% overall and by up to 23.3% on rare-entity slices. We release MERLIN-Rare, rare-entity test slices for targeted evaluation, with our framework.

</details>


### [41] [Multilingual in Name Only? Cultural and Linguistic Weaknesses of LLMs in Urdu](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.10758)
*Farah Adeeba,Abdul Rafae Khan,Rajesh Bhatt,Hassan Sajjad*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文评估多语言大语言模型在低资源语言乌尔都语故事生成中的可靠性，发现其存在明显的语言、语义、连贯性与文化问题，少样本提示也难以有效修复。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多语言大语言模型正被广泛用于开放式文本生成，但其在低资源语言中的生成质量、事实与文化可靠性仍缺乏系统研究。本文选择乌尔都语作为代表，旨在评估模型生成故事时的语言正确性、语义合理性、篇章连贯性和文化适切性。

Method: 作者使用三种当代大语言模型（GPT-5.1、Qwen-3-Max、DeepSeek-3.1）生成93篇乌尔都语故事，构建 Urdu-Stories 语料库；随后依据涵盖语言、语义和文化问题的九类标注体系进行人工错误标注，并进一步采用少样本提示，检验提示策略能否缓解文化与上下文错误。

Result: 模型生成内容频繁出现基础语法和语义错误；故事整体连贯性不足，存在不自然的重复，并表现出普遍的文化肤浅性。少样本提示对文化错误和上下文错误的改善有限，相关问题大多仍然存在。

Conclusion: 当前多语言大语言模型尚不能作为低资源语言中可靠的故事生成器或信息获取来源。对于乌尔都语等低资源语言，模型评估不能只关注表面流畅度，还应系统考察语法、语义、篇章连贯性、文化适切性与上下文一致性。

Abstract: Multilingual large language models (LLMs) are increasingly used for open-ended text generation, yet their behaviour in low-resource languages remains poorly understood. In this work, we question how correct and reliable is the generation of multilingual LLMs when used for the task of story generation. We consider Urdu language as a representative low-resource language. We generate Urdu-Stories, a corpus of 93 stories generated using three contemporary LLMs (GPT-5.1, Qwen-3-Max, DeepSeek-3.1). We manually annotate the errors present in them under a nine-label linguistic, semantic, and cultural taxonomy. Our notable findings suggest that LLMs often make basic errors of grammar and semantics. The stories lack coherence, have unnatural repetition and show pervasive cultural shallowness. We further show using few-shot prompting that the cultural and context errors largely remain unresolved. Our findings highlight the limitations of current LLMs as a reliable source of content generation and information retrieval for low-resource languages.

</details>


### [42] [Analyzing Traditional and Neural Approaches to Multilingual Readability Assessment](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.10792)
*Joshua Wong,Chris Tanner*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文研究多语种Transformer模型是否学习了传统可解释可读性模型所依赖的语言学特征。作者利用SHAP提取传统分类器的重要特征，再通过TCAV探测XLM-R及各语言专用编码器中的相关概念。结果显示，Transformer能够恢复长度、句法和词汇多样性等信号，并部分体现CEFR等级结构；但对齐程度受模型、语言和层数影响，语言专用模型通常比XLM-R更接近传统模型。研究还发现，线性可分性并不必然意味着特征具有方向性影响。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 尽管Transformer在自动可读性评估中表现优异，传统特征模型仍具有重要价值，因为其预测能够对应到明确的语言学属性。可读性标签具有主观性且依赖评分者，单纯追求对噪声标签的高准确率，可能只是在捕捉表面模式，而非真正反映文本难度的语言结构。因此，本文旨在检验多语种Transformer是否内化了传统可读性模型使用的语言学特征，并分析这种特征对齐在不同语言、模型和网络层之间的差异。

Method: 研究使用覆盖阿拉伯语、英语、法语、印地语和俄语的ReadMe++数据集。首先，使用SHAP识别传统特征分类器对预测最重要的特征；随后将这些特征构造成TCAV概念集，用于探测多语种XLM-R和语言专用编码器内部的相关概念表示。研究进一步比较模型对表面长度、句法、词汇多样性及CEFR序数结构的编码情况，并区分概念的线性可分性与其方向性影响。

Result: Transformer模型能够恢复传统模型中的表面长度、句法和词汇多样性信号，并体现传统模型所反映的CEFR等级顺序结构。但特征对齐程度因模型家族、语言和层数而异；总体而言，语言专用编码器比多语种XLM-R更清晰地追踪传统模型。研究还发现，某一特征在表示空间中具有较高线性可分性，并不意味着该特征对模型输出具有稳定的方向性影响，这对使用线性探针对计数型可读性特征进行解释提出了限制。

Conclusion: 多语种Transformer并非完全依赖不可解释的表面模式，而是能够在一定程度上内化传统可读性模型所使用的语言学结构。然而，这种内化具有模型、语言和层级依赖性，且不能仅凭线性可分性判断特征是否真正影响预测。结合SHAP与TCAV能够更细致地评估神经模型与可解释语言学特征之间的对应关系。

Abstract: Transformer-based models excel at Automatic Readability Assessment (ARA), yet feature-based models remain in active use because their predictions tie back to linguistic properties. This matters because readability labels are subjective and rater-dependent, so high accuracy on noisy ground truth may reflect surface patterns rather than the linguistic structure that defines difficulty. We test whether transformers internalize the same features as traditional models across Arabic, English, French, Hindi, and Russian using the ReadMe++ dataset. Shapley Additive Explanations (SHAP) identify the features driving traditional classifiers, which we then use as TCAV concept sets to probe multilingual XLM-R and language-specific encoders. Transformers recover surface-length, syntactic, and lexical-diversity signals, and reflect the ordinal CEFR structure of the traditional models. Alignment varies by model family, language, and layer, with language-specific encoders tracking traditional models more clearly than XLM-R. High linear separability does not always imply directional influence, limiting linear probing for count-based readability features.

</details>
